At the core of EPPO is a task-wise dynamic clipping mechanism that replaces the fixed clipping threshold in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a task entropy-aware adaptive bound, tightening updates for over-confident tasks while relaxing them for under-explored ones. Experiments on the multi-task agentic benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed EPPO yields results superior to its counterparts.

</details>


### [181] [Predicting LLM Safety Before Release by Simulating Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07184)
*Marcus Williams,Hannah Sheahan,Cameron Raymond,Tomek Korbak,Deng Pan,Peilin Yang,Leon Maksin,Ningyi Xie,Phillip Guo,Ian Kivlichan,Micah Carroll*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"部署模拟"方法：从先前模型部署的去标识化对话中提取初始前缀，用候选模型重新生成后续响应，从而在未发布前估计模型在实际部署中的不良行为率和发现新的对齐问题。在GPT-5系列部署中的评估显示，该方法能有效预测部署后不良行为率，优于传统评估方法，且可从公开聊天数据中播种，为外部研究者提供无私有日志的部署评估途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统部署前安全评估存在覆盖不足、缺乏代表性、可识别性强等问题，难以反映模型在实际部署中的真实行为频率。作者旨在通过模拟真实部署环境来更准确地预测模型在现实世界中的不良行为发生率，从而为发布决策提供更有力的定量证据。

Method: 利用先前模型部署中已去标识化的完整对话，固定对话的初始前缀（用户输入及模型已有响应），然后使用候选模型重新生成下一个响应。这些生成的响应可用于审计新的对齐问题，并估计不良行为在部署前的发生率。该方法在GPT-5系列四个部署上进行了评估，包括对GPT-5.4的注册、结果盲化预测，以及对三个早期发布的回顾性分析。

Result: 部署模拟能产生信息量丰富的部署后不良行为率估计，在性能上优于基于对抗性选择的生产数据的基线方法。其评估感知点估计比传统评估更接近实际生产流量。工具重采样的真实感是进一步提升预测的主要挑战，但即使在复杂工具使用场景中这一挑战也可克服。此外，从公开聊天数据播种的部署模拟仍能对生产不良行为率保持信息量，表明外部研究者无需私有生产日志即可进行部署评估。

Conclusion: 部署模拟为评估者提供了一种预测语言模型在现实世界中行为的方法，支持更定量的部署风险评估。该方法弥补了传统预部署评估与现实部署表现之间的差距，为更安全、更负责任地发布AI模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Pre-deployment safety evaluations aim to inform the downstream risks of releasing a new AI model. Yet most evaluations provide limited evidence about how often undesired model behavior will occur in deployment: they generally have insufficient coverage, are unrepresentative, and are generally recognizable as tests. To address these concerns, we study a simple way to simulate a model deployment: starting from de-identified conversations from a previous model deployment, we hold fixed the initial conversation prefix and regenerate the next response using a candidate model. The resulting responses can then both be audited for novel misalignments and used to estimate the prevalence of model misbehavior before deployment. We evaluate deployment simulation across four GPT-5-series deployments, using registered, outcome-blinded predictions for GPT-5.4 and retrospective analyses of three earlier releases. We find that deployment simulation produces informative estimates of post-deployment misbehavior rates and outperforms baselines based on adversarially selected production data; its evaluation-awareness point estimates were also much closer to production traffic than those from traditional evaluations. We also identify the realism of tool resampling as a central challenge for further improving predictions and share results suggesting that this challenge is surmountable even in complex tool-use settings. Finally, we show that deployment simulation can be seeded from public chat datasets and remain informative about production misbehavior rates, suggesting a path for external researchers to run deployment-grounded evaluations without access to private production logs. Overall, deployment simulation helps evaluators forecast how language models will behave in the real world and supports more quantitative assessment of deployment risk.

</details>


### [182] [Geometric--Nongeometric Optimizer Calculus: A Modular Language for Reachable Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07206)
*Zavier Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何-非几何优化器演算（geometric-nongeometric optimizer calculus），一种模块化语言，用于在显式预算、状态和规则约束下审计可达的梯度方法。主要理论结果是方向表达性定理：远离临界点时，完全正定几何精确表达严格下降方向。论文还定义了受限方向残差、精确表达性条件，并将方向级诊断与条件数几何复杂度分离。最终的设计问题是一个关于模块预算的帕累托优化，而非单一通用优化器排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应优化器混合了多种机制（度规/预条件、估计、记忆、步长控制、约束、随机性、目标修改、离散化），但缺乏统一的模块化语言来分析这些组件如何协同工作以及它们能表达哪些下降方向。作者希望建立一个形式化的框架来审计和比较不同优化器组件的能力边界。

Method: 提出几何-非几何优化器演算。几何模块是一个正定余度量族，将协向量映射到参数空间方向；非几何模块包括信息、记忆、控制、算子、噪声、目标和离散化机制。通过方向表达性定理分析不同几何配置下可表达的方向集合，定义方向残差度量，并将问题转化为模块预算的帕累托优化。

Result: 完全正定几何能精确表达所有严格下降方向；推导了对角线和块状几何的精确表达性条件；将方向残差与条件数几何复杂度分离；提出了轨迹级残差复杂度概念；高信息全度规探针在确定性二次基准上达到数值精度，Muon风格的PyTorch候选在小规模上展示了矩阵-算子更新可被该演算审计。

Conclusion: 优化器设计应是模块预算的帕累托优化问题，而非寻找单一最优排序。该框架为理解和审计优化器组件提供形式化语言，但本文不宣称大规模SOTA性能，主要贡献在理论和基准语言层面。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers mix several mechanisms: a metric or preconditioner maps gradients to descent directions, while estimation, memory, step-size control, constraints, stochasticity, target modification, and discretization determine which directions are available and how they are used. We introduce geometric--nongeometric optimizer calculus, a modular language for auditing reachable gradient methods under explicit oracle, budget, state, and rule constraints. The geometric module is a positive cometric family that maps covectors to parameter-space directions; the nongeometric modules are information, memory, control, operator, noise, target, and discretization mechanisms. The main formal result is a direction-expressivity theorem: away from critical points, full positive-definite geometry expresses exactly the strict descent directions. We then define restricted direction residuals for admissible metric families, prove exact expressivity conditions for diagonal and block geometries, and separate this direction-level diagnostic from condition-number geometric complexity. The resulting design problem is a Pareto optimization over module budgets, not a single universal optimizer ordering. We also lift pointwise residuals to a trajectory-level residual complexity that couples direction mismatch with the variation of the explaining geometry. We include diagnostic prototypes only as evidence for the language: a high-information full-metric probe solves deterministic quadratic benchmarks to numerical precision, while a practical Muon-style PyTorch candidate gives small-scale evidence that matrix-operator updates can be audited by the calculus. The paper is a theory and benchmark-language manuscript; it does not claim large-scale optimizer state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [183] [HPG-Diff: Hierarchical physics-guided diffusion with differentiable connectivity constraints for topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07233)
*Jinbo Yang,Mingyue Yuan,Boyuan Zhang,Yoshifumi Kitamura,Shikai Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为层次化物理引导扩散（HPG-Diff）的框架，通过两种协同机制——层次化物理引导策略和浮动材料抑制损失——在拓扑优化中强制执行物理一致性，显著提升了泛化能力和拓扑连通性，在分布内和分布外场景下分别实现了0.87%和5.29%的平均合规误差，并将浮动材料比率降至约2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生成模型在拓扑优化中缺乏内在物理引导，导致对未见边界条件的泛化能力差，并产生浮动材料伪影。作者旨在解决这两个关键问题，提升生成拓扑结构的物理一致性和实用性。

Method: 提出HPG-Diff框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 层次化物理引导策略，将预计算的不同物理特征与去噪过程对齐，指导材料分布朝向最优载荷路径；(2) 浮动材料抑制损失，利用热传导启发的可微连接性约束，通过模拟从载荷位置的虚拟热传播过程在训练中显式惩罚浮动材料。

Result: HPG-Diff实现了分布内平均合规误差0.87%、分布外5.29%；浮动材料比率分别降至2.90%和2.44%。在3:1矩形域（悬臂梁和桥基准）上的案例研究初步表明，使用小数据集的轻量级LoRA微调可支持HPG-Diff适应矩形非方形域。

Conclusion: HPG-Diff通过物理引导和连接性约束有效提升了生成拓扑结构的质量，在泛化能力和拓扑连通性上均优于现有方法，且通过LoRA微调可适应不同域形状，具有较好的实用潜力。

Abstract: Deep generative models offer a promising paradigm for topology optimization, enabling rapid design exploration. However, these approaches lack intrinsic physics guidance, often leading to poor generalizability across unseen boundary conditions and the formation of floating material artifacts. To address these limitations, we propose Hierarchical Physics-Guided Diffusion (HPG-Diff), a novel diffusion framework that enforces physics consistency through two synergistic mechanisms. First, we introduce a hierarchical physics-guided strategy that aligns different precomputed physics features with the denoising process, guiding material distribution toward optimal load paths to enhance generalizability. Second, we propose a floating material suppression loss as a differentiable connectivity constraint inspired by thermal conduction to improve topological connectivity. By simulating a virtual heat propagation process from load positions, this mechanism explicitly penalizes floating material during training. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate that HPG-Diff achieves average compliance errors of 0.87% (in-distribution) and 5.29% (out-of-distribution), while reducing floating material ratios to 2.90% and 2.44%, respectively. Furthermore, case studies on a 3:1 rectangular domain, including cantilever and bridge benchmarks, provide preliminary evidence that lightweight LoRA fine-tuning with a small dataset can support the adaptation of HPG-Diff to rectangular non-square domains.

</details>


### [184] [ORCAID: Oblique Rule-Based Continuous-Action Interpretation for Deep RL Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07235)
*Ignacio D. Lopez-Miguel,Ezio Bartocci,Thomas Eiter,Martin Tappler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORCAID是一种从连续动作空间的强化学习智能体中提取可解释规则化策略的方法，通过高效斜决策树训练算法（随机初始化、局部优化、反向消除三阶段分裂搜索）与相邻叶子合并，生成简洁的规则集，在保持强性能的同时参数少，并能提升原始深度RL策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习的可解释性仍是关键问题，特别是在连续动作空间中从复杂环境训练的智能体中提取可解释策略极具挑战性。

Method: 提出ORCAID方法：1) 高效斜决策树训练算法，通过超平面划分状态空间并拟合局部线性模型；2) 三阶段分裂搜索：随机初始化、局部优化、反向消除；3) 合并相邻叶子生成简洁可解释规则集。

Result: 在多个RL环境中，ORCAID提取的规则化策略维持了强性能且参数数量低，并能用于提升原始深度RL策略的性能。

Conclusion: ORCAID有效解决了连续动作空间下RL策略的可解释性提取问题，生成的规则化策略性能强、参数少，且具有提升原始策略的额外价值。

Abstract: Explainability remains a key issue in reinforcement learning (RL). Distilling an interpretable policy from an agent trained in a complex environment is particularly challenging when the action space is continuous. We introduce ORCAID, a novel method for extracting interpretable rule-based policies from RL agents operating in mixed continuous-discrete environments with continuous action spaces. Our main contribution is an efficient oblique decision tree training algorithm that partitions the state space by hyperplanes and fits local linear models. The key idea lies in a three-stage split search: efficient random initialization, local refinement, and backward elimination. Finally, adjacent leaves are merged to yield a concise set of interpretable rules describing a given deep RL policy. We evaluate ORCAID across multiple RL environments, demonstrating that the extracted rule-based policies maintain strong performance with a low number of parameters and can even be used to improve the performance of the original deep RL policy.

</details>


### [185] [Safe Reinforcement Learning using Ideas from Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07252)
*Georg Schäfer,Jakob Rehrl,Stefan Huber,Simon Hirlaender*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用框架，将深度强化学习（DRL）的自适应高性能与模型预测控制（MPC）的形式化安全保障相结合。通过离线MPC计算定义可行状态-动作空间，在训练和部署阶段利用安全滤波器将RL智能体的即时动作投影到该全局验证的可行集上，从而确保主动学习阶段的严格安全约束。在非线性1-DoF实验室平台上验证了该方法在物理硬件上的成功探索与稳定策略收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在复杂信息物理系统与机器人领域应用中面临的核心挑战：如何在主动学习阶段确保严格的硬安全约束。在真实物理系统中，违反机械限制可能导致不可逆损伤，因此探索必须严格限制在安全运行区域内。

Method: 提出一种通用框架：1) 利用系统动力学数学模型进行离线MPC计算，定义可行状态-动作空间；2) 在训练和部署阶段，通过安全滤波器将RL智能体的即时动作投影到全局验证的可行集上；3) 结合DRL的自适应高性能与MPC的形式化安全保障。

Result: 在非线性1-DoF（单自由度）实验室测试平台上进行系统评估，成功实现了物理硬件上的安全探索与稳定策略收敛。

Conclusion: 该通用框架能够有效解决RL在主动学习阶段的硬安全约束问题，在真实物理系统上验证了方法的可行性和有效性，为安全关键型CPS和机器人应用提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) enables the synthesis of control policies directly from data, making it highly appealing for complex cyber-physical systems (CPSs) and robotics. A persistent challenge, however, is ensuring strict, hard safety constraints during the active learning phase. In real-world physical systems, violating mechanical limits can cause irreversible damage, necessitating that exploration remains strictly within safe operational regions. We propose a generalized framework that combines the adaptive, high-performance nature of deep reinforcement learning (DRL) with the formal safety guarantees of model predictive control (MPC). Using a mathematical model of the system dynamics, offline MPC computations define a feasible state-action space, representing all safe combinations of system states and control inputs that guarantee constraint satisfaction. During training and deployment, the RL agent's instantaneous actions are projected onto this globally verified feasible set via a safety filter. We systematically evaluate our generalized approach on a non-linear 1-DoF laboratory testbed, demonstrating successful exploration and stable policy convergence on physical hardware.

</details>


### [186] [FMMVCC: Fuzzy Mamba-based Multi-View Contrastive Clustering for Univariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07258)
*Donato Cerciello,Leonardo Schiavo,Angel Panizo-LLedot,Javier Huertas Tato,David Camacho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FMMVCC，一种基于Mamba的深度时间序列聚类框架，利用状态空间序列建模实现线性复杂度的时序表征学习，并结合多视图自监督学习（时间掩码与数据增强）。在15个基准数据集上，FMMVCC在60项指标评估中取得29项最佳，平均排名最高，持续优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中时间序列数据量大但标注稀缺，监督方法难以应用，需依赖无监督聚类。现有深度聚类方法难以捕获长程时序依赖，或计算成本过高，亟需一种高效且能建模长期依赖的无监督时间序列聚类方法。

Method: 提出FMMVCC框架，核心创新包括：(1) 采用Mamba（状态空间序列模型）进行时序表征学习，实现线性复杂度的高效长程依赖建模；(2) 引入多视图自监督学习，通过时间掩码与数据增强构建对比学习任务，提升表征质量。

Result: 在15个基准数据集、60项指标评估中，FMMVCC取得29项最佳性能，在所有测试场景中平均排名最高，持续优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: FMMVCC通过Mamba的高效序列建模与多视图自监督学习，有效解决了时间序列无监督聚类中长程依赖建模与计算效率的平衡问题，为大规模时序数据分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: In many realistic scenarios, large volumes of time series data are generated with limited or expensive annotations. This limitation makes supervised learning methods difficult to apply and leads to the use of unsupervised approaches capable of discovering meaningful structures directly from raw data. Clustering therefore plays a crucial role in organizing time series into groups that share similar temporal patterns, enabling exploratory analysis and downstream tasks without requiring manual labeling. However, existing deep clustering methods often struggle to capture long-range temporal dependencies or rely on architectures with high computational cost. This paper introduces FMMVCC, a Mamba-based deep clustering framework for time series that leverages state space sequence modeling to efficiently learn temporal representations with linear complexity. Additionally, it utilizes multi-view self-supervised learning with temporal masking and augmentations. Experimental evaluation in 15 benchmark datasets proves that FMMVCC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving the best overall performance in 29 of 60 total metric evaluations and the highest average rank in all tested scenarios.

</details>


### [187] [BubbleSH: A Dataset of Rising Bubbles with Deformable Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07275)
*Rachna Ramesh,Kiet Bennema ten Brinke,Douwe Orij,Ivo Roghair,Vlado Menkovski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BubbleSH是一个由高保真直接数值模拟生成的瞬态三维气泡群数据集，采用球谐函数紧凑表示气泡形态，旨在支持数据驱动的可变形气泡流建模，并提供了评估轨迹和形状预测的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气泡流具有复杂的多尺度动力学特性，可变形气泡通过与周围液体相互作用产生强耦合的运动与形态行为。传统方法难以捕捉这种复杂动态，需要高质量数据集来推动数据驱动建模。

Method: 通过高保真直接数值模拟在周期域中模拟气泡上升过程，提供时间分辨的气泡轨迹、速度和形状演化数据，使用球谐函数紧凑表示气泡形态，并设计了轨迹和形状预测的评估指标。

Result: 成功构建了一个轻量级但物理表达力强的数据集，并验证了置换和平移等变概率仿真器在该数据集上的有效性，证明了其对生成式模型（学习未来轨迹分布）的适用性。

Conclusion: BubbleSH为可变形混沌多相系统的数据驱动模型开发与评估提供了一个紧凑、高保真的数据集和基准，特别适合需要学习未来轨迹分布的生成式模型。

Abstract: Bubbly flows exhibit complex multiscale dynamics, with deformable bubbles interacting through the surrounding liquid and giving rise to strongly coupled kinematic and morphological behavior. We present BubbleSH, a bubbly flows dataset consisting of transient, three-dimensional bubble-swarm dynamics obtained from high-fidelity direct numerical simulations of bubbles rising in a periodic domain. The dataset provides time-resolved bubble trajectories, velocities, and shape evolution, with bubble morphology compactly represented using spherical harmonics. Designed to be lightweight yet physically expressive, the dataset enables data-driven modeling of bubbly flow simulators where shape deformation and bubble-bubble interactions play a central role. We characterize the dataset with bubble kinematics, morphology, and interaction patterns, and introduce evaluation metrics for both trajectory and shape prediction. The sensitivity of bubble-swarm dynamics to local perturbations makes BubbleSH particularly well suited to generative models that learn distributions over possible future trajectories. We evaluate a permutationally and translationally equivariant probabilistic emulator on BubbleSH given the proposed metrics. Therefore, we establish a compact, high-fidelity dataset and a benchmark for developing and evaluating data-driven models of deformable, chaotic multiphase systems.

</details>


### [188] [Nonlinear Bandit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07304)
*Tianshuo Zheng,Ting Wu,Zhi-Hua Zhou,Keqin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对重尾噪声下的广义线性赌博机（GLB）问题，提出基于在线镜像下降（OMD）的自适应Huber损失算法EHM，实现近乎最优的遗憾界；并进一步拓展到分段常数上下文特征场景（PGLB-EHM）及非线性赌博机（NB-EHM），通过仿射提升方法获得次线性遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾分布广泛存在于个性化推荐、金融市场、医疗等实际应用中，而现有GLB算法多假设噪声为轻尾或次高斯分布，难以处理重尾噪声带来的鲁棒性问题；同时希望减少算法对先验参数的依赖。

Method: 1) 基于在线镜像下降（OMD）与自适应Huber损失，提出EHM算法，单轮更新计算复杂度为O(1)；2) 利用链接函数的特殊性质消除对常用参数的需求；3) 针对分段常数上下文特征场景，改进得到PGLB-EHM算法；4) 针对非线性赌博机，结合二分法与特殊约束提出NB-EHM算法，并利用仿射提升方法将一般NB问题归约到该框架。

Result: EHM算法在重尾噪声下达到近乎最优的遗憾界$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{\frac{1}{1+ε}})$；PGLB-EHM保持相同的遗憾阶数；NB-EHM在仿射提升方法下获得次线性遗憾界。

Conclusion: 本文系统地解决了重尾噪声下GLB及NB的遗憾最小化问题，所提算法兼具计算高效性（单步O(1)）与理论最优性，并为非线性赌博机提供了可行的归约框架。

Abstract: In this paper we first study the problem of generalized linear bandit (GLB) under heavy-tailed noise. The characteristics of heavy-tailed distributions are widely observed in real-world applications such as personalized recommendation, financial markets, and medical treatments. Based on the online mirror descent (OMD) method, we propose an algorithm EHM that extends the adaptive Huber loss method (Wang et al., 2025) with one-pass update ($\mathcal{O}(1)$ computational complexity with respect to current round $t$ and the time horizon $T$), which simultaneously achieves an almost optimal regret of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{\frac{1}{1+ε}})$ where $T$ is the time horizon. In addition, by utilizing a special property of some link function (Sawarni et al., 2025), our algorithm eliminates the need to know a commonly used parameter. Next, we study the GLB problem under the case when contextual characteristic becomes piecewise constant, and we slightly revised former algorithm to obtain the PGLB-EHM algorithm. After theoretical analysis, we prove that the regret upper bound order stays the same. Furthermore, we look deeper into a special case of nonlinear bandit (NB) and present the NB-EHM algorithm with bisection method and special restriction. Eventually we utilize the affine lifting approach and show that the general NB problem can be applied with NB-EHM to achieve a sublinear regret bound.

</details>


### [189] [FedCVESA: Taking Away Training Data in Federated Learning via Correlation Value Encoding and Segmented Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07314)
*Chongkai Li,Bang Zhang,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCVESA，一种联邦学习环境下的白盒TATD攻击方法。恶意服务器通过皮尔逊相关正则化器将目标客户的私有训练数据编码到模型的载体参数中，并采用分段聚合策略防止参数被覆盖，从而在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上成功窃取训练图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的集中式TATD攻击利用深度模型的记忆能力存储和恢复训练数据，但该威胁在联邦学习（FL）环境下尚未被系统研究。FL中多客户端平均聚合可能覆盖编码的训练数据，因此需要研究FL场景下的参数级记忆通道攻击。

Method: 提出FedCVESA方法：1）在目标客户端损失函数中添加皮尔逊相关正则化器，将私有训练数据逐步编码到选定的载体参数中；2）采用分段聚合策略，在服务器聚合时保留选定的载体参数，仅对剩余参数进行标准平均，以减少载体参数被覆盖的风险。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上，采用狄利克雷非独立同分布划分进行实验。结果表明该方法能够从训练完成的模型中窃取具有语义意义的私有训练图像，同时在受控的概念验证设置下保持可接受的主任务效用。

Conclusion: 实验证明联邦学习在研究的白盒恶意服务器设置下，可以成为主动TATD攻击的参数级记忆通道，揭示了FL环境下的新型隐私泄露风险。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) avoids explicit data exposure by keeping raw data on local clients, yet privacy risks remain in the training process and the learned model itself. Recently, centralized Taking Away Training Data (TATD) attacks have shown that malicious training could abuse the memorization capacity of deep models to store and later recover training data. However, this memorization-based threat has not been systematically studied under FL environments, where multi-client averaging could overwrite encoded training data. In this paper, we study a white-box TATD attack in which a malicious server selects n target clients from K participating clients and actively writes private training data into the global model during federated training. We propose FedCVESA, a federated variant of Correlation Value Encoding Attack (CVEA), by adding a Pearson-correlation regularizer to the loss function of target clients, so that private training data are gradually encoded into selected model parameters, referred to as carrier parameters. To reduce the overwriting of carrier parameters during server aggregation, we further propose segmented aggregation over dispersed carrier parameters, preserving selected carrier parameters while keeping standard averaging on the remaining parameters. Experiments on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 under Dirichlet non-IID partitions show that the proposed method can steal semantically meaningful private training images from the trained model while maintaining acceptable main-task utility in a controlled proof-of-concept setting. These results demonstrate that FL can become a parameter-level memorization channel for active TATD attack under the studied white-box malicious-server setting.

</details>


### [190] [Mechanistic Interpretability for Neural Networks: Circuits, Sparse Features and Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07316)
*Pranav Sawant,Jakub Krejčí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文全面回顾了机制可解释性这一新兴领域，旨在对现代神经网络内部算法进行逆向工程。它超越了传统XAI的表层输入-输出相关性分析，直接解决机器学习模型的"黑箱"问题，这对高风险部署中的安全性和可审计性至关重要。文章详细考察了Transformer电路分析，探讨了残差流、注意力机制和归纳头如何驱动复杂任务和上下文学习，随后解决了叠加和多语义性的核心挑战，展示了稀疏自编码器和转码器如何将纠缠的网络激活分解为可解释的特征。文章还探索了通过引导向量和因果干预主动控制和修改模型行为的方法，最后将这些机制见解与神经符号AI框架联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可解释AI方法仅停留在表面输入-输出相关性分析，无法真正揭示现代神经网络内部算法的工作原理。随着机器学习模型在高风险领域（如医疗、金融、司法）的广泛应用，理解模型内部运作机制对于确保安全性、可审计性和可靠性变得至关重要。研究者希望通过逆向工程的方式打开神经网络的"黑箱"，从而更好地控制、调试和信任这些模型。

Method: 本文采用综述性研究方法，系统性地梳理了机制可解释性的主要技术路线，包括：(1) Transformer电路分析——剖析残差流、注意力机制和归纳头等内部组件如何驱动复杂任务；(2) 稀疏自编码器(SAEs)和转码器——解决叠加和多语义性挑战，将纠缠的网络激活分解为可解释特征；(3) 引导向量和因果干预——主动控制和修改模型行为的方法；(4) 神经符号AI框架——将神经表示转化为显式可执行的逻辑规则。

Result: 本文呈现了机制可解释性领域的主要技术进展和研究成果：成功解析了Transformer内部组件的功能角色（如归纳头驱动上下文学习）；证明了稀疏自编码器能够有效分解多语义神经元中的叠加特征；展示了通过引导向量和因果干预可以实现对模型行为的精确控制；以及初步建立了从神经表示到符号逻辑规则的转化路径。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性为理解和控制现代神经网络提供了一个强有力的技术框架，它通过逆向工程直接揭示了模型内部算法，从而弥补了传统可解释AI方法的不足。该领域的研究对于提升高风险场景中AI系统的安全性和可信度具有重要意义，并为未来实现更透明、更可控的AI系统奠定了基础。研究者对未来前景持乐观态度，认为这些技术将推动AI系统向更加可解释、可审计的方向发展。

Abstract: This article offers a comprehensive overview of mechanistic interpretability, an emerging field that seeks to reverse-engineer the internal algorithms of modern neural networks. While traditional explainable AI methods often stop at surface-level input-output correlations, this approach directly addresses the opaque "black box" nature of machine learning models, which is essential for ensuring safety and auditability in high-stakes deployments. The paper provides a detailed examination of Transformer circuit analysis, exploring how internal components like the residual stream, attention mechanisms, and induction heads drive complex tasks and in-context learning. It subsequently tackles the core challenge of superposition and polysemanticity, demonstrating how tools like Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) and transcoders can decompose tangled network activations into distinct, human-interpretable features. Furthermore, the paper explores methods for actively controlling and modifying model behavior through steering vectors and causal interventions. Finally, it connects these mechanistic insights with neurosymbolic AI frameworks designed to translate neural representations into explicit, executable logical rules.

</details>


### [191] [Hypergraph Neural Stochastic Diffusion: An SDE Framework for Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07330)
*Zhiheng Zhou,Mengyao Zhou,Dengyi Zhao,Xingqin Qi,Guiying Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyperNSD，一种基于随机微分方程的超图不确定性估计框架，将超图表示建模为随机过程，通过轨迹变异性量化不确定性，在保持预测精度的同时实现可靠的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图神经网络的预测不确定性尚未充分探索，现有方法主要从最终预测估计不确定性或依赖计算昂贵的集成和贝叶斯推理，无法捕捉表示学习过程中的不确定性演化。此外，超图的不确定性不仅来自噪声属性与模糊标签，还来自节点-超边关联结构的变化和复杂高阶依赖。

Method: 提出HyperNSD框架，将超图表示建模为在节点-超边关联结构上演化的随机过程。可学习的漂移函数捕捉确定性高阶扩散动力学，可学习的随机强迫函数刻画结构模糊性与表示噪声。通过随机表示轨迹的变异性直接量化预测不确定性，实现预测与不确定性传播的联合学习。

Result: 理论分析证明了所提出随机动力学的适定性、扰动稳定性、置换等变性和数值收敛性。在多个超图基准上，HyperNSD在分布外检测和误分类检测中实现了可靠的不确定性估计，同时保持了有竞争力的预测精度。

Conclusion: 为可信的高阶表示学习提供了原则性的随机动力学框架，推动了超图神经网络不确定性估计的发展。

Abstract: Hypergraph neural networks have shown powerful capability in modeling higher-order relations, yet their predictive uncertainty remains underexplored. Unlike pairwise graphs, uncertainty in hypergraphs arises not only from noisy attributes and ambiguous labels, but also from variations in node-hyperedge incidence structures and complex higher-order dependencies. Existing approaches mainly estimate uncertainty from final predictions or rely on computationally expensive ensembles and Bayesian inference, limiting their ability to capture uncertainty evolution during representation learning. In this paper, we propose Hypergraph Neural Stochastic Diffusion(HyperNSD), a stochastic differential equation framework for uncertainty estimation on hypergraphs. HyperNSD models hypergraph representations as stochastic processes evolving over node-hyperedge incidence structures. A learnable drift function captures deterministic higher-order diffusion dynamics, while a learnable stochastic forcing function characterizes structural ambiguity and representation noise. Predictive uncertainty is directly quantified through the variability of stochastic representation trajectories, providing an intrinsic uncertainty measure beyond post-hoc confidence scores. We formulate HyperNSD with neural drift and diffusion networks, enabling joint learning of prediction and uncertainty propagation. Theoretical analyses establish well posedness, perturbation stability,permutation equivariance, and numerical convergence of the proposed stochastic dynamics. Experiments on multiple hypergraph benchmarks demonstrate that HyperNSD achieves reliable uncertainty estimation for out-of-distribution and misclassification detection while preserving competitive prediction accuracy. These results provide a principled stochastic-dynamical framework for trustworthy higher-order representation learning.

</details>


### [192] [Latency-Aware Bid Acceptance under Operational Feasibility: A Public Benchmark with Hindsight Ceilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07343)
*Aswin Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FreightBidBench，一个公开可校准、无依赖的闭环基准测试，用于在线卡车货运竞标接受问题。该基准显式建模了运营可行性、车队重定位成本和社会成本，并基于公共数据源构建。作者开发了两种全知 hindsight 上限（LP松弛和Lagrangian信息松弛），后者在紧张和稀缺运力场景下分别比LP松弛严格20.7%和39.3%。他们还提出了参数化代理-rollout级联策略，在10个随机种子的实验中，最佳简单策略保留了rollout利润的91.0%和86.5%，标准库代理保留了94.2%和89.3%。单级级联在40-56%的决策延迟下恢复了约98%的rollout利润，在紧张场景下与rollout教师统计上无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线卡车货运竞标接受是一个闭环随机决策问题，需要实时接受或拒绝运输订单，同时考虑运营可行性、车队重定位成本和社会成本。然而，现有的公开可复现的基准测试很少：现有的路由基准是静态的，而动态车队研究通常依赖私有运营数据。这阻碍了该领域的可复现研究和算法比较。

Method: 作者构建了FreightBidBench基准，基于Freight Analysis Framework和美国农业部卡车费率数据等公共数据源。开发了两种全知hindsight上限：简单LP风格松弛和更严格的Lagrangian-per-truck信息松弛。提出了参数化代理-rollout级联策略，包含边界带和稀缺压力升级触发器。

Result: Lagrangian信息松弛在紧张运力场景下比LP松弛严格20.7%，在稀缺运力场景下严格39.3%。最佳简单策略保留了rollout利润的91.0%和86.5%，标准库代理保留了94.2%和89.3%。单级级联在40-56%的决策延迟下恢复了约98%的rollout利润，在紧张场景下与rollout教师统计上无显著差异。

Conclusion: FreightBidBench提供了一个公开、可复现的闭环基准测试，填补了该领域公开基准的空白。提出的代理-rollout级联策略在保持高决策质量的同时显著降低了计算延迟，为实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Online truckload bid acceptance is a closed-loop stochastic decision problem in which a carrier or broker must, in real time, accept or reject a tendered load subject to operational feasibility, fleet repositioning costs, and opportunity cost against future demand. Public, reproducible benchmarks for this problem are scarce: existing routing benchmarks are static, while dynamic-fleet studies typically rely on private operator data. We introduce FreightBidBench, a public-calibrated, dependency-free, closed-loop benchmark in which feasibility (pickup reach, appointment windows, simplified hours-of-service, stochastic yard delays) and economics (service-failure penalty, terminal fleet value, daily price-premium window) are explicit, versioned, and reproducible from public Freight Analysis Framework and U.S. Department of Agriculture truck rate data. We develop two full-horizon hindsight ceilings: a simple LP style relaxation and a tighter Lagrangian-per-truck information relaxation that retains per-truck hours-of-service and sequencing structure and is 20.7% tighter than the LP relaxation on a tight-capacity scenario and 39.3% tighter on a scarce-capacity scenario. We introduce a parametric surrogate-rollout cascade with boundary-band and scarcity-pressure escalation triggers. On ten-seed tight and scarce scenarios, the best simple policy retains 91.0% and 86.5% of rollout profit and the standard-library surrogate 94.2% and 89.3%; a cascade at a single escalation band recovers roughly 98% on both at 40-56% of rollout's mean decision latency, and on the tight scenario is statistically indistinguishable from the rollout teacher (paired-bootstrap 95% CI on the profit delta spans zero).

</details>


### [193] [Multi-Agent AI Control: Distributed Attacks Hamper Per-Instance Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07368)
*Oliver Makins,Orazio Angelini,Zohreh Shams,Mary Phuong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究多智能体AI控制问题，构建了FakeLab合成AI实验室代码库，发现“碎片化效应”：随着攻击智能体数量增加，单智能体监控捕获每个攻击者的概率降低。显式规划器会放大该效应，使攻击完成率提升高达七倍。强监控模型虽可将未检测成功降低一个数量级以上，但仍不完美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制研究聚焦于单智能体单轨迹场景，但实际部署中多智能体共享基础设施，最严重风险（如模型权重窃取、训练投毒）需要多个智能体协同完成。因此需要将AI控制研究扩展到多智能体场景。

Method: 构建FakeLab合成AI实验室代码库（含9个服务、86个良性任务、4种攻击目标），系统评估单智能体监控在不同智能体数量、协调方式、模型能力及监控配置下的表现。

Result: 发现三个核心结果：(1) 碎片化效应：随攻击智能体增加，每个攻击者被捕获概率降低；(2) 该效应并非由良性与恶意代码比例驱动，而更依赖于模型能力；(3) 显式规划器将攻击完成率提升高达七倍，并强化了碎片化效应；弱监控模型漏检大多数攻击，强监控模型可将未检测成功降低一个数量级以上但仍不完美。

Conclusion: 多智能体场景下的AI控制面临碎片化效应带来的新挑战，需要开发更强的监控机制和防御策略。研究团队愿与安全研究者共享FakeLab及控制设置。

Abstract: AI control is a family of techniques to prevent an AI with malicious goals from subverting its operator's intent. AI Control usually studies a single agent in one trajectory, but real deployments run many agents over shared infrastructure, and the most severe risks (model-weight exfiltration, training-run poisoning) plausibly need several agents acting in concert. We initiate the empirical study of multi-agent AI control, formalising distributed attacks in which several agents jointly aim for a malicious goal. We develop FakeLab: a synthetic AI-lab codebase (9 services, 86 benign tasks, 4 attack objectives). We evaluate single agent monitoring against distributed attacks, varying the number of agents, their coordination, model capabilities and precise monitoring configuration.
  Our central finding is the fragmentation effect: as more agents coordinate to attack, per-agent monitoring becomes less likely to catch any of the attackers. Secondly, this is not driven by the ratio of benign to malicious code -- we argue this is likely dependent on model capability. Third, an explicit planner amplifies the fragmentation effect, raises the rate of completing the attack up to sevenfold, and mildly sharpens executors' poor monitor-theory-of-mind. Finally: a weak "trusted" monitor misses most attacks, while a stronger one cuts undetected success by more than an order of magnitude but remains imperfect. We are happy to share FakeLab, our control setting, with safety researchers on request.

</details>


### [194] [On Adversarial Vulnerability of Vision-Language Models through the Lens of Intermediate Spectral Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07375)
*Chethan Krishnamurthy Ramanaik,Tobias Callies,Michael Hecht,Eirini Ntoutsi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于谱子空间引导的白盒攻击方法SSGRA，通过将中间表示对齐到底部右奇异向量张成的子空间，揭示并利用视觉-语言模型中线性变换层的谱结构脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗脆弱性研究主要从决策边界几何、特征鲁棒性、输入-输出雅可比矩阵和逆问题不稳定性等角度展开，但忽略了现代DNN中传播信息的中间线性变换的谱结构这一未被探索的脆弱性机制。

Method: 提出白盒谱子空间引导攻击（SSGRA），使中间表示与底部右奇异向量张成的子空间对齐，利用视觉-语言模型中线性层的可解释谱分解特性。

Result: 实验表明SSGRA在攻击有效性上优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: SSGRA不仅提供了更有效的攻击方法，还为视觉-语言模型中的对抗脆弱性提供了谱解释，为改进模型鲁棒性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Adversarial vulnerability in deep neural networks (DNNs) has been studied from the perspectives of decision-boundary geometry, feature robustness, input-output Jacobians, and the instability of inverse problems. Here, we focus on the spectral structure of intermediate linear transformations that propagate information through modern DNNs, an unexplored mechanism of adversarial vulnerability. Specifically, we investigate transformer-based vision-language models, whose linear layers admit interpretable spectral decompositions and whose widespread adoption makes understanding their robustness increasingly important. We propose a white-box spectral-subspace-guided attack (SSGRA) that aligns intermediate representations with the subspace spanned by the bottom right singular vectors. Our experiments show improved attack effectiveness over existing baselines. In addition, SSGRA offers a spectral interpretation of adversarial vulnerability in VLMs, providing insights for improving their robustness.

</details>


### [195] [Generalist Vision-Language Models for Fast Radio Burst detection: a zero-shot benchmark against a specialized detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07382)
*Raiff H. Santos,Amilcar R. Queiroz,Tharcisyo S. S. Duarte,K. E. L. de Farias,Rafael A. Batista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了小型、开源、本地运行的通才视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在零样本、仅靠提示（prompt）的模式下检测快速射电暴（FRB）的能力，无需微调或标注样本。在模拟动态谱数据集上，Gemma 4 2B 达到了与专用检测器 SwinYNet 相当的准确率（93.65% vs 92.90%），且对结构射频干扰（RFI）的误报率显著更低（6.4% vs 25.0%），对纯噪声无误报。通过改写提示即可将同一模型重新配置为三分类任务，准确率达86%且无任何虚假FRB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用深度学习检测器需要大量任务特定训练数据且无法在不重新训练的情况下重新定义，而通才VLM可能通过零样本方式灵活适配FRB检测任务，降低对标注数据和专用模型重训的依赖。

Method: 使用两个Gemma 4系列VLM（2B和4B参数），在零样本、仅提示模式下对模拟L波段动态谱进行二分类（FRB vs 非FRB）和三分类（FRB/RFI/噪声）检测，并与专用检测器SwinYNet进行逐样本对比。

Result: 在2000样本的平衡基准上，Gemma 4 2B默认阈值下准确率93.65%（SwinYNet为92.90%），对结构RFI误报率6.4%（SwinYNet为25.0%），对纯噪声无误报；SwinYNet的ROC-AUC为1.0000（VLM为0.9482）。三分类任务中VLM最高准确率86%，无虚假FRB。

Conclusion: 小型通才VLM在零样本条件下可达到与专用检测器可比的FRB检测准确率，尤其在抑制误报方面表现出色，并且通过提示工程可灵活切换任务，展示了通才模型在科学数据分析中的潜力。

Abstract: Fast Radio Bursts (FRBs) are millisecond-duration radio transients whose automated detection increasingly relies on highly specialized deep learning models. These detectors achieve exceptional performance, but they require large task-specific training datasets and cannot be redefined without retraining. In this work, we evaluate whether small, open-weight, locally run generalist Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can detect FRBs in dynamic spectra under a zero-shot, prompt-only regime, with no fine-tuning and no labeled examples, returning structured decisions with a natural-language justification. From a controlled set of 3000 simulated L-band dynamic spectra containing FRBs, structured Radio Frequency Interference (RFI), and noise, we draw a balanced binary benchmark of 2000 samples and compare two such VLMs (Gemma 4 2B and 4B), sample by sample, against the state-of-the-art specialized detector SwinYNet. At the default threshold, Gemma 4 2B reaches an accuracy of 93.65%, with no statistically significant difference from SwinYNet (92.90%), while showing a significantly lower false-positive rate on structured RFI (6.4% vs. 25.0%) and no false positives on pure noise. SwinYNet retains a perfect probabilistic ranking on this benchmark (ROC-AUC of 1.0000 vs. 0.9482), a ceiling that the zero-shot VLM approaches from general-purpose pretraining alone. Rewriting the prompt alone reconfigures the same models for three-class FRB/RFI/noise classification on the full set of 3000 spectra, where they reach up to 86% accuracy without a single false FRB.

</details>


### [196] [Sparse Delta Memory: Scaling the State of Linear RNNs through Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07386)
*Loïc Cabannes,Pierre-Emmanuel Mazaré,Gergely Szilvasy,Matthijs Douze,Maria Lomeli,Ilze Amanda Auzina,Justin Carpentier,Gabriel Synnaeve,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sparse Delta Memory (SDM)架构，通过稀疏寻址方案将门控线性RNN的隐藏状态容量提升数个数量级，在isoFLOP约束和相同参数量下，更高的状态记忆容量显著提升了上下文学习和长上下文检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型因状态大小有限，在长上下文召回任务上落后于基于softmax注意力的Transformer架构。增加状态大小虽能提升召回性能，但代价是更高的FLOPs。因此需要在不显著增加计算量的前提下提高模型的状态容量。

Method: SDM基于Gated DeltaNet架构，用稀疏读写操作替代密集的键值外积，通过稀疏寻址方案访问大规模显式记忆，从而将隐藏状态容量扩展数个数量级。

Result: 在isoFLOP约束和相同参数量下，更高的状态记忆容量显著提升了上下文学习和长上下文检索任务的性能。通过学习SDM记忆的初始状态作为参数化记忆，模型在常识知识和推理任务上也有进一步提升。

Conclusion: SDM通过稀疏寻址实现了大容量显式记忆与高效计算的平衡，在多种任务上表现优于传统线性注意力模型，验证了状态容量对于长上下文建模的重要性。

Abstract: Linear attention models allow a fixed state size and a fixed amount of compute per token. However, due to their limited state size, linear attention models fall behind in long-context recall compared to softmax-attention-based transformer architectures. Increasing the state size of linear attention improves recall performance but at the cost of higher FLOPs. In this work, we introduce Sparse Delta Memory (SDM), an architecture that scales the hidden state of gated linear RNNs to orders of magnitude higher capacity using a sparse addressing scheme. SDM extends the Gated DeltaNet architecture by replacing the dense key-value outer product with sparse reads and writes to a large explicit memory. We show that, under an isoFLOP constraint and with an identical number of parameters, a higher state memory capacity significantly improves performance on in-context learning and long-context retrieval tasks. Moreover, by learning the initial state of the SDM memory and therefore using it as a parametric memory, we show that the model further improves on a wide range of common-knowledge and reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [197] [The Optimal Sample Complexity of Learning Autoregressive Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07423)
*Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在可实现PAC设置下，完整自回归思维链（Chain-of-Thought）轨迹的精确追踪学习的样本复杂度，由局部下一个词元类的标准多类学习率（由Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz维数控制）给出上界。尽管精确追踪损失下单个错误动作会导致整个轨迹错误，但样本复杂度与 rollout 长度无关，仅依赖于DS维数和置信参数。论文引入奇偶维数（parity dimension）——一种基于偶伪立方体的rollout稳定DS维数细化，并证明了DS维数在rollout下可能增长，因此需要这一绕行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chain-of-Thought（CoT）推理中，模型需要生成一系列中间步骤（token）才能到达最终答案。在精确追踪损失下，只要一个步骤错误，整个轨迹就被判定为错误。这引发了一个基本问题：CoT推理的样本复杂度是否随轨迹长度增长？传统直觉认为长轨迹会需要更多样本，但本文旨在证明样本复杂度实际上与rollout长度无关，仅由局部预测类的复杂度决定。

Method: 本文通过引入"奇偶维数"（parity dimension）——一种基于偶伪立方体的DS维数的rollout稳定细化，来证明样本复杂度上界。具体地：（1）利用低坐标生成定理（low-coordinate spanning theorem）在有限限制上控制一包含密度；（2）证明奇偶维数在自回归rollout下不会增加（与DS维数不同）；（3）以此推导出样本复杂度为O((DSdim(H)+log(1/δ))/ε)，完全消除了对rollout长度的依赖。

Result: 对于每个停止规则halt和每个点态halt-停止局部类H，在精确追踪损失下，Roll_{halt}(H)的PAC样本复杂度为O((DSdim(H)+log(1/δ))/ε)，与rollout长度无关。对DSdim(H)的依赖是worst-case最优的，因为单步停止可还原为H的普通多类学习。论文还证明了DS维数在rollout下可能增长，从而说明引入奇偶维数是必要的。

Conclusion: CoT推理的精确追踪学习在样本复杂度上不随推理轨迹长度增长，这为长链推理的可学习性提供了理论保证。奇偶维数作为DS维数的rollout稳定版本，为分析自回归序列学习提供了更合适的复杂度度量。这一发现对理解大语言模型中CoT能力的样本效率有重要理论意义。

Abstract: We prove that, in the realizable PAC setting, the sample complexity of exact-trace learning for full autoregressive Chain-of-Thought traces is upper bounded by the standard multiclass rate of the local next-token class, where this rate is governed by the Daniely--Shalev-Shwartz dimension. Under exact-trace loss, one wrong action makes the whole trace incorrect; nevertheless, for every stopping rule $\mathtt{halt}$ and every pointwise $\mathtt{halt}$-halting local class $\mathrm{H}$, $n_{\mathrm{PAC}}^{\varepsilon,δ}(\operatorname{Roll}_{\mathtt{halt}}(\mathrm{H}))=O((\operatorname{DSdim}(\mathrm{H})+\log(1/δ))/\varepsilon)$, with no dependence on rollout length. The dependence on $\operatorname{DSdim}(\mathrm{H})$ is worst-case optimal, since one-step stopping recovers ordinary multiclass learning of $\mathrm{H}$. The proof introduces parity dimension, a rollout-stable refinement of DS dimension based on even pseudo-cubes. It controls one-inclusion density via a low-coordinate spanning theorem on finite restrictions and, unlike DS dimension itself, does not increase under autoregressive rollout. We also show why this detour is necessary: DS dimension can increase under rollout.

</details>


### [198] [RLVP: Penalize the Path, Reward the Outcome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07435)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出针对真实世界代理任务（如拨打电话）的路径惩罚+结果奖励的强化学习方法，解决RLVR无法处理路径约束和样本效率低下的问题，实现高任务成功率并接近零违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的代理（如代为拨打电话）必须在代价高昂且不可逆的真实交互中在线学习，而非廉价仿真环境。这带来两大挑战：第一，可部署性不仅取决于最终结果，还取决于路径——必须遵守结果中性的约束（如不反复拨打无人接听的电话、遵守营业时间、完成身份验证），而基于结果的奖励无法表达这些约束，因为频繁违规反而会提高表面成功率；第二，每次交互成本高昂，代理必须从极少样本中高效学习。标准RLVR对这两个挑战视而不见：它仅优化结果产出，并且将所有失败组的组内相对优势归零，浪费昂贵的交互轮次。

Method: 提出"惩罚路径，奖励结果"的配方。核心洞察：由于组内相对优势等价于组内方差，密集信号只有在它能提供结果所缺乏的方差时才有帮助。路径上的可验证惩罚能可靠地满足这一条件，而进展势函数仅在部分进展可达时才有用。具体给出四条有效惩罚的设计规则，包括避免单独使用惩罚时出现的"不作为陷阱"。

Result: 该方法实现了高任务成功率并接近零违规，而仅基于结果的训练几乎在每个回合都会违反约束。

Conclusion: 在真实世界代理任务中，通过在输出端奖励结果同时沿路径施加可验证惩罚，可以同时满足路径约束遵守和高效样本学习的要求，优于纯结果优化的RLVR方法。

Abstract: Agents acting on our behalf in the real world (e.g. placing phone calls) must learn online from costly, often irreversible interactions rather than cheap simulator steps. Two things follow. First, deployability depends on the path, not only the outcome. An agent must respect outcome-neutral constraints such as not repeatedly calling an unresponsive user, respecting business hours, or completing required authentication constraints that outcome-based rewards cannot express, since violating them frequently improves apparent success. Second, because each interaction is expensive, the agent must learn efficiently from very few examples.
  Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) is blind to both challenges: it optimizes solely on the outcome and wastes expensive rollouts on all-fail groups where group-relative advantage collapses to zero. Attempts to densify supervision by rewarding progress target the hard-to-verify direction. In contrast, real agentic environments can cheaply detect bad moves. Since group-relative advantage is equivalent to within-group variance, a dense signal helps only when it supplies variance the outcome lacks. A verifiable penalty on the path meets this condition reliably, while a progress potential helps only where partial progress is reachable. The resulting recipe "penalize the path, reward the outcome" achieves high task success with near-zero violations, where outcome-only training violates constraints on nearly every episode. We provide four design rules for effective penalties, including avoidance of the inaction trap that arises when a penalty is used in isolation.

</details>


### [199] [Where to Intervene? Benchmarking Fairness-Aware Learning on Differentially Private Synthetic Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07471)
*Vinícius Gabriel Angelozzi,Héber H. Arcolezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了差分隐私（DP）合成数据下的公平性干预措施，发现DP会加剧公平性损失，但后处理方法能在不同隐私预算下实现更稳定的公平-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作分别研究了差分隐私和公平性，但二者目标可能冲突：DP常放大群体间差异，且公平性干预在DP约束下是否仍然有效尚不明确。

Method: 以AIM作为最先进的边际DP合成器，在四个数据集上评估三种缓解策略（预处理、处理中、后处理），比较四种流水线配置（Baseline、DP-only、Fair-only、DP+Fair），覆盖多个群体公平性指标和广泛的隐私预算范围。

Result: DP单独使用会降低效用和公平性；公平性干预可部分恢复公平结果；后处理方法在不同隐私预算和合成器下提供更稳定的公平-效用权衡，在实现强公平性提升的同时保持竞争性效用。

Conclusion: 后处理公平干预在DP合成数据场景下最具鲁棒性，为隐私-公平-效用的权衡提供了实践指导；开放源代码和实验成果以促进可复现研究。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains, raising concerns about both privacy and fairness. Differential Privacy (DP) has become a gold standard for privacy-preserving data analysis, while fairness-aware mechanisms aim to mitigate discrimination against underrepresented groups. However, these objectives can conflict: DP often amplifies disparities across demographic groups, and little is known about whether established fairness interventions remain effective under DP constraints. In this work, we present, to our knowledge, the first systematic evaluation of fairness interventions on differentially private synthetic tabular data. Our benchmark centers on the Adaptive Iterative Mechanism (AIM), identified as the state-of-the-art marginal-based DP synthesizer (Cormode et al. 2025). We thus evaluate fairness interventions across four datasets, multiple group fairness metrics, and three categories of mitigation strategies (pre-processing, in-processing, and post-processing) under a wide range of privacy budgets. We compare four pipeline configurations: (Baseline) training on original data; (DP-only) training on DP synthetic data; (Fair-only) applying fairness mechanisms on original data; and (DP+Fair) combining fairness mechanisms with DP synthetic data. Our results demonstrate that while DP alone can degrade both utility and fairness, applying fairness interventions can partially restore equitable outcomes. Among them, post-processing methods tend to provide more stable fairness-utility trade-offs across privacy budgets and synthesizers, achieving strong fairness improvements while preserving competitive utility relative to other intervention stages. We release all code, data, and experimental artifacts in an open-source repository to ensure full reproducibility and to support future research on the privacy-fairness-utility trade-off.

</details>


### [200] [Selective Timestep Weighting and Advantage-Based Replay for Sample-Efficient Diffusion RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07693)
*Eric Zhu,Abhinav Shrivastava,Soumik Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补策略（逐时间步加权和优先回放机制）显著提升扩散模型RLHF的反馈效率，在相同超参数下实现最高6倍样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型RLHF方法需要大量人类或奖励模型评估，反馈效率极低，限制了其在真实场景中的应用。

Method: 1) 逐时间步加权方案：根据去噪过程中不同时间步对奖励信号的贡献差异进行加权，理论上与PPO最优收敛性关联；2) 优先回放机制：优先利用信息量丰富的轨迹，避免重复查询新奖励，实现样本复用。

Result: 在相同超参数设置下，该方法相比广泛使用的扩散RLHF基线实现了最高6倍的样本效率提升。

Conclusion: 通过识别奖励信息在扩散轨迹中分布不均的特性，利用信息性时间步和轨迹的加权优化，可大幅提升扩散模型RLHF的反馈效率，同时保持对未见提示的泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for aligning generative models with human preferences. However, applying RLHF to diffusion models remains highly feedback inefficient, as existing approaches typically require large amounts of human or reward model evaluations. This limitation reduces the practicality of diffusion RLHF in realworld settings where feedback is the primary bottleneck. In this paper, we propose two complementary strategies that substantially improve the feedback efficiency of diffusion RLHF while preserving generalization to unseen prompts. Our key observation is that reward information in diffusion trajectories is unevenly distributed: not all denoising timesteps or trajectories contribute equally to learning from a reward signal. By emphasizing informative timesteps and trajectories during optimization, we obtain more effective gradient updates. First, we introduce a per-timestep weighting scheme that reweights denoising steps during policy optimization. We theoretically connect this weighting to the optimal convergence properties of proximal policy optimization (PPO) and approximate the resulting weighting trend empirically. Second, we introduce a replay mechanism that prioritizes informative trajectories, enabling the model to reuse past samples instead of repeatedly querying new rewards. Together, these strategies significantly improve the feedback efficiency of diffusion RLHF. Under identical hyperparameter settings, our approach achieves up to a 6$\times$ improvement in sample efficiency compared to widely used diffusion RLHF baselines.

</details>


### [201] [Reward-Adaptive Iterative Discovery: A Case Study on Automated Game Testing for NHL26](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07498)
*Florian Fuchs,Jessy Gosselin-Grant,Boris Skuin,Michele Petteni,Alessandro Sestini,Joakim Bergdahl,Amir Baghi,Linus Gisslén*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RAID方法，利用迭代强化学习自动发现游戏AI漏洞，在NHL 26中找到6种与人类测试员发现相似的得分策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏行业测试成本高昂，人类测试员需要耗费大量时间反复测试AI行为漏洞

Method: 基于强化学习的RAID方法，通过训练多样化进球智能体种群，引入简单扩展使RL算法发现多种高质量解决方案

Result: 单次实验即发现6种冰球得分漏洞策略，在质量上与人类测试员数小时手动测试发现的结果相似

Conclusion: RAID方法可有效替代部分人工测试工作，降低游戏AI漏洞检测的人力成本

Abstract: Testing is a major effort for the gaming industry, requiring a significant part of development budget and people power. We present a case study on a development version of the ice hockey game EA SPORTS NHL 26, for which human playtesters test the goalie AI for behavioral exploits. To reduce the effort of re-testing the goalie AI after every game or behavior modification in the development phase, we propose Reward-Adaptive Iterative Discovery (RAID), a novel approach to automatically find exploits using an iterative Reinforcement Learning (RL) approach that trains a population of goal scoring agents. While previous approaches can already successfully find exploits, RL algorithms tend to overfit to a single solution. We introduce a simple extension on top of existing RL algorithms, such that they find multiple diverse high-quality solutions. For our first deployment of this approach, within a single experiment we were able to find six hockey scoring exploit strategies that were qualitatively similar to those that playtesters had found in hours-long manual testing sessions.

</details>


### [202] [TimEE: End-to-end Time Series Classification via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07500)
*Jaris Küken,Shi Bin Hoo,Martin Mráz,Frank Hutter,Lennart Purucker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimEE 是一个 4.5M 参数的基础模型，采用端到端上下文学习（ICL）进行时间序列分类（TSC）。它通过元学习在纯合成任务上训练，无需在每个数据集上训练，就能直接在单个前向传播中输出分类分布。尽管从未见过真实数据，它在 UCR 基准上取得了 ROC AUC 第一、准确率第三的成绩，成为首个仅通过合成预训练达到 SOTA 的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列分类主流范式是两阶段方法（先训练特征编码器再训练分类器），存在三个痛点：（1）表示学习与分类目标分离，优化不协同；（2）每个新数据集都需要重新训练，效率低；（3）推理时无法利用标签信息。因此，本文旨在设计一种端到端、无需每数据集训练、能在推理时利用支持集标签信息的时间序列分类模型。

Method: 基于 Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFN) 框架，TimEE 在纯合成生成的时间序列分类任务上进行元学习。每个合成任务包含具有不同类别身份的时间序列，这些类别通过生成过程中的结构化分布偏移来区分。模型架构为 4.5M 参数的 Transformer，以支持集（带标签）和查询时间序列作为输入，直接输出预测的类别分布，通过单次前向传播完成分类，无需额外的分类头或每数据集训练。

Result: 在 UCR 基准测试中，TimEE 在与所有对比方法（包括基础模型和有监督深度学习基线）的比较中，ROC AUC 排名第一，准确率排名第三。这是首个仅通过合成数据预训练就在 UCR 上达到最先进性能的模型，证明了纯合成预训练的上下文学习在时间序列分类中的有效性。

Conclusion: 端到端上下文学习结合合成先验是时间序列分类的一个有前景且尚未充分探索的方向。未来工作可从扩展模型规模、改进先验设计、引入更丰富的生成机制等方向继续提升。

Abstract: Time series classification (TSC) is dominated by a two-stage paradigm: train a feature encoder -- either from scratch on the target dataset or via pretraining on large corpora -- and then fit a task-specific classifier on top. While effective, this decoupling optimizes representation learning independently of the classification objective, requires per-dataset training, and prevents the model from exploiting label information during inference. We introduce TimEE, a 4.5M-parameter foundation model for end-to-end TSC via in-context learning. Given a labeled support set and a query time series, TimEE directly outputs a predicted class distribution in a single forward pass with no per-dataset training required. Following the prior-data fitted network (PFN) framework, TimEE is meta-trained exclusively on synthetic TSC tasks, where each task contains time series with distinct class identities arising from structured distributional shifts in the generative process. Despite seeing no real time series during pre-training, TimEE ranks first in ROC AUC (and third on accuracy) on the UCR benchmark among all compared methods, which include both foundation models and supervised deep learning baselines. To our knowledge, TimEE is the first purely synthetic-pretrained model to reach state-of-the-art performance on the UCR benchmark. These results establish end-to-end ICL with synthetic priors as a compelling, largely unexplored direction for TSC, with scaling, prior design, and richer generation mechanisms as natural avenues for improvement. Code is publicly available at http://github.com/automl/timee.

</details>


### [203] [Single-Rollout Asynchronous Optimization for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07508)
*Zhenyu Hou,Yujiang Li,Jie Tang,Yuxiao Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAO是一种单采样异步强化学习方法，通过单路径采样替代分组采样以减少off-policy问题，并引入严格的双侧token级裁剪策略提升训练稳定性，在编码和推理任务上持续超越GRPO等基线，并成功部署于GLM-5.2模型训练中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步RL系统过于强调吞吐量，忽视了训练稳定性和任务有效性。GRPO框架中的分组采样不适用于异步agentic训练，导致严重的off-policy问题和不稳定性。

Method: 提出SAO框架：(1) 单路径采样：每个prompt仅使用一个rollout替代传统分组采样，减少off-policy效应并提升泛化能力；(2) 引入实用的价值模型训练设计；(3) 严格的双侧token级裁剪策略提升优化稳定性。

Result: SAO能稳定训练一千步，在SWE-Bench Verified、BeyondAIME、IMOAnswerBench等编码和推理基准上持续优于GRPO及其变体。单采样RL在模拟在线学习（模型需适应动态环境）中尤为有效。已成功部署于GLM-5.2（750B-A40B）的agentic RL训练管线。

Conclusion: 单采样异步优化SAO有效解决了异步RL中off-policy和不稳定性问题，为长程agentic任务的强化学习训练提供了更高效且稳定的替代方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is becoming increasingly important for post-training large language models (LLMs). Previous RL pipelines for LLMs were mostly synchronous and batch-interleaved, which is inefficient for long-horizon agentic tasks. Recently, asynchronous RL has emerged as a more efficient alternative by updating the model as rollouts arrive. However, existing asynchronous RL systems often emphasize throughput, while leaving training stability and task effectiveness largely underexplored. For example, a key challenge is that group-wise sampling in the widely-used GRPO framework does not naturally fit asynchronous agentic training. In this paper, we present Single-rollout Asynchronous Optimization (SAO) to address the stability and off-policy challenges in asynchronous RL. To reduce off-policy effects and improve generalization, we replace group-wise sampling with single-rollout sampling, that is, using one rollout per prompt. We further improve this single-rollout strategy with practical value-model training designs. To improve optimization stability, we introduce a strict double-side token-level clipping strategy. SAO is able to train stably for one thousand steps and consistently outperform GRPO and its variants on agentic coding and reasoning benchmarks, such as SWE-Bench Verified, BeyondAIME, and IMOAnswerBench. We also demonstrate that single-rollout RL is particularly effective in a simulated online learning setting, where the model must adapt to changing evolving environments. To this end, SAO is successfully deployed in the agentic RL pipeline for training the open GLM-5.2 model (750B-A40B).

</details>


### [204] [Fast Rates for Semi-Supervised Learning via Data-Augmentation Graph Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07513)
*Adam M. Oberman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上解释了自监督学习在少量标注样本下能达到高准确率的原因，证明其泛化误差以标签数 $n_L$ 的 $O(1/n_L)$ 速率衰减，远优于监督学习的 $O(1/\sqrt{n_L})$，并将误差上界分解为 $C/n_L + R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$，其中 $R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$ 衡量数据增强跨越标签边界的程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习在少量标注样本上已能媲美全监督学习的准确率，但这种标注样本高效性缺乏理论解释。

Method: 将数据增强构建的相似图视为未标注数据上的图拉普拉斯正则化学习问题，将 Johnson & Zhang 的留一法稳定性工具迁移到增强图框架下，提出一个简化的损失函数（去除投影头、负样本和正交性等冗余组件），并推导出快速传递学习速率。

Result: 证明了 $O(1/n_L)$ 的传递学习速率（监督学习为 $O(1/\sqrt{n_L})$）；误差上界为 $C/n_L + R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$，其中 $R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$ 是数据增强跨越标签边界的图割质量；在无限数据极限下能恢复增强核的特征空间。

Conclusion: 自监督学习的标注效率来源于数据增强诱导的图结构提供的强归纳偏置，好的增强策略可以大大减少所需标签数量；该理论直接解释准确率与标签数的曲线关系，而非仅泛化间隙的边界。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning matches supervised accuracy from a fraction of the labels, but the labeled-sample efficiency behind this has lacked a theoretical explanation. We provide one. Data augmentation induces a similarity graph on the unlabeled data, so downstream learning on that graph is graph-Laplacian-regularized learning. We prove a fast transductive rate, $O(1/n_L)$ in the number of labels, in place of the supervised $O(1/\sqrt{n_L})$, by carrying the leave-one-out stability apparatus of Johnson and Zhang (JMLR 2007) over to the augmentation graph, and without the unrealistic assumptions of limit-based analyses (exact kernel, generalizing features). The bound makes augmentation quality explicit: the expected error is at most $C/n_L + R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$, where the data-augmentation alignment error $R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$ is the graph-cut mass of augmentations that cross a label boundary, so good augmentations let few labels suffice. The analysis uses a streamlined loss that drops the projector, negative-sample, and orthogonality overhead of standard objectives yet still recovers the top-$K$ ideal features in the infinite-data limit, the augmentation-kernel eigenspace studied by Zhai et al. The result explains the observed accuracy-versus-label-count curve rather than only bounding a generalization gap.

</details>


### [205] [Gradient-free Riemannian Langevin Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07519)
*Ricardo Baptista,Olivier Zahm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需梯度的黎曼Langevin采样器（GRiLS），通过引入黎曼度量重塑局部几何结构，促进多模态分布中的模式间跳转，改善了MCMC的混合效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法在多模态概率分布采样中常面临混合不良和模式捕获问题，尤其是当目标密度的梯度不可用或计算昂贵时，现有方法效果不佳。

Method: 提出无需梯度的黎曼Langevin采样器（GRiLS），引入黎曼度量重塑局部几何结构以促进模式间跳转，利用目标密度的均值和协方差（通过交互粒子集合估计）构建提议分布，无需梯度计算。

Result: 在多模态基准测试上的实验结果表明，GRiLS相较于现有的基于梯度和无需梯度的MCMC方法，实现了更好的混合性能。

Conclusion: GRiLS是一种高效的无梯度MCMC算法，特别适用于梯度不可用或不切实际的复杂、计算昂贵的目标分布，能有效缓解多模态采样中的模式捕获和混合不良问题。

Abstract: We address the problem of efficiently sampling multimodal probability distributions, where standard Markov Chain Monte Carlo methods often suffer from poor mixing and mode trapping. To mitigate these issues, we propose Gradient-free Riemannian Langevin Sampler (GRiLS), a novel proposal that improves exploration without requiring gradient evaluations of the target density. Our approach introduces a Riemannian metric which reshapes the local geometry in order to facilitate transitions across modes. The resulting gradient-free MCMC algorithm is particularly suitable for complex, computationally expensive targets where derivatives are unavailable or impractical. The GRiLS proposal requires knowing the mean and covariance of the target density, which we estimate using an ensemble of interacting particles. Empirical results on multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that GRiLS achieves improved mixing compared to existing gradient-based and gradient-free MCMC approaches.

</details>


### [206] [Avoiding unsafe sets when training with Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07538)
*Adam M. Oberman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带噪声梯度下降训练过程中，轨迹落在指定失效区域的概率上界问题。在光滑强凸损失函数下，给出了三个概率界：平衡态质量指数小、沿轨迹的形状无关界以及基于局部松弛率的改进界。揭示了强凸性决定训练松弛速度，但失效区域的几何形状决定轨迹是否会在途中“鼓包”穿过该区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全验证需求：在带噪声梯度下降（过阻尼Langevin动力学）训练模型中，需要约束轨迹落在指定失效区域$\mathcal{A}_H$的概率$\nu_t(\mathcal{A}_H)$，以确保训练过程的安全性。

Method: 将带噪声梯度下降理想化为过阻尼Langevin动力学，利用光滑强凸损失函数的全局谱隙$m$，建立三个概率界：（1）平衡态质量$\pi(\mathcal{A}_H)$的指数小界；（2）沿轨迹的形状无关界$\nu_t(\mathcal{A}_H) \le \pi(\mathcal{A}_H)(1 + \sqrt{\chi_0^2/\pi(\mathcal{A}_H)}\,e^{-mt})$；（3）针对几何孤立区域，通过中心化指示函数的谱测度定义局部松弛率，结合最大值原理得到一致上界。

Result: 三个概率界：平衡态质量$\pi(\mathcal{A}_H)$在$d$维中指数小，且小噪声下具有互补的能量势垒率；轨迹形状无关界显示集内概率在$O(d)$的预热时间后松弛到静态值的两倍；Ornstein-Uhlenbeck示例表明预热时间必要——平衡壳的角切片可能瞬态膨胀指数$d$倍；几何孤立区域的局部松弛率超过全局率，可成比例缩短预热时间。

Conclusion: 强凸性决定了训练松弛的速度，但失效区域的几何形状决定了轨迹是否会在返回最小值的途中“鼓包”穿过该区域。局部几何结构对瞬态概率行为起关键作用。

Abstract: Training a model with noisy gradient descent can be idealized as overdamped Langevin dynamics on the loss landscape, and a natural safety question is to bound the probability $ν_t(\mathcal{A}_H) = \mathbb{P}(Q_t \in \mathcal{A}_H)$ that the trajectory lies in a designated failure region $\mathcal{A}_H$. We study this for a smooth, strongly convex loss in $d$ dimensions and a failure region separated from the minimizer by an energy gap. Three bounds emerge. At the end of training, the equilibrium mass $π(\mathcal{A}_H)$ is exponentially small in $d$, with a complementary energy-barrier rate when the noise is small. Along the trajectory, a shape-free bound $ν_t(\mathcal{A}_H) \le π(\mathcal{A}_H)(1 + \sqrt{χ_0^2/π(\mathcal{A}_H)}\,e^{-mt})$ shows that the in-set probability relaxes to (twice) the static value after a burn-in time of order $d$, using only the global spectral gap $m$ of the loss. A worked Ornstein-Uhlenbeck example shows this burn-in is necessary: an angular slice of the equilibrium shell can transiently swell by a factor exponential in $d$, even though its equilibrium mass is tiny. To rule such swelling out we introduce a local relaxation rate attached to the failure region, defined through the spectral measure of its centered indicator rather than a Dirichlet-form Rayleigh quotient. For geometrically isolated regions this rate exceeds the global one, shrinking the burn-in proportionally, and combined with a maximum-principle ceiling it caps the trajectory probability uniformly in time. The picture is that strong convexity sets how fast training relaxes, but the shape of the unsafe set decides whether the trajectory bulges through it on the way home.

</details>


### [207] [Collaborative Synthetic Data Generation for Knowledge Transfer in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07565)
*Maximilian Andreas Hoefler,Karsten Mueller,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedKT-CSD是一个基于神经图像压缩思想的一轮联邦学习框架，利用预训练的公共自编码器作为共享潜在空间，通过类条件潜在统计量聚合、安全聚合和差分隐私噪声添加，在保证低通信开销、鲁棒性处理数据异质性以及严格隐私保护的前提下，生成合成数据集用于训练全局模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一轮联邦学习方法大多缺乏正式的隐私保证，无法同时实现低通信开销、对数据异质性的鲁棒性以及严格的隐私保护。

Method: 利用公开预训练的自编码器作为共享潜在空间，每个客户端通过单次前向传递编码私有数据并计算类条件潜在统计量；服务器通过安全聚合聚合这些统计量，添加校准的差分隐私噪声，然后解码生成合成数据集用于训练全局模型。

Result: 在隐私约束下，FedKT-CSD在多种数据集和异质性设置下能够与无隐私保护的基线方法竞争甚至超越，并能扩展到大量客户端。

Conclusion: FedKT-CSD通过巧妙结合预训练自编码器、安全聚合和差分隐私技术，成功填补了一轮联邦学习在隐私保证方面的空白，实现了通信效率、数据异质性鲁棒性和严格隐私保护三者兼得。

Abstract: One-shot federated learning (OSFL) addresses the communication overhead of federated learning by limiting training to a single round, but doing so without sacrificing model quality is non-trivial, particularly when client data distributions diverge. Recent work has addressed this challenge by aggregating client knowledge on the server through the construction of transferable synthetic datasets or distillates. However, most of these methods lack formal privacy guarantees, leaving a gap in jointly achieving low communication, robustness to heterogeneity, and rigorous privacy. We propose FedKT-CSD (Federated Knowledge Transfer via Collaborative Synthetic Data), a framework inspired by neural image compression that closes this gap by leveraging publicly pretrained autoencoders as a shared latent space. Each client encodes its private data in a single forward pass, computes class-conditional latent statistics, and transmits these to the server. The server aggregates these statistics via secure aggregation, adds calibrated differential privacy noise, and decodes a synthetic dataset for training a global model and further downstream tasks. This design provides formal $(\varepsilon,δ)$-differential privacy by construction, while keeping client-side computation and communication lightweight. Despite operating under privacy constraints, FedKT-CSD is competitive with and even outperforms non-private baselines across diverse datasets and heterogeneity settings, and scales to a large number of clients. Our code is available at: https://github.com/an7123/FedKT-CSD

</details>


### [208] [Multi-Class vs. Multi-Label BERT for CVE-to-CWE Mapping: How Taxonomy Structure Shapes the Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07573)
*Ana Schwengber Kelm,Christian Bockermann,Jörg Frochte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将CVE记录自动分类到CWE类别的问题，比较了多类（单标签）和多标签两种分类范式，在三种Transformer编码器（BERT Base、SecureBERT、CySecBERT）和三种标签空间（83、47、25类）上进行评估。结果表明多类训练在所有设置下均获得更高的macro-F1，但随着标签空间缩小差距从21%缩小到2%；后处理阈值优化可消除25类设置下的差距。错误分析显示主导性错误模式遵循CWE层次结构且与编码器选择无关（Pearson r > 0.92），表明错误结构更多由分类法设计驱动。层次宽松评估将macro-F1从~81%提升至~90%，表明严格指标低估了分支级分类器质量。CySecBERT在多标签设置中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将CWE类别分配给CVE记录是漏洞分析中重要但主要依赖人工的步骤，自动化这一过程具有重要的实际意义。

Method: 将该任务形式化为文本分类问题，比较多类（单标签）和多标签两种建模选择；使用三种Transformer编码器（BERT Base、SecureBERT、CySecBERT）在三种嵌套标签空间（83、47、25类）上进行评估；采用后处理阈值优化和层次宽松评估方法分析分类性能与错误结构。

Result: 多类训练在所有设置下macro-F1更高，与多标签的差距从21%缩小到2%（标签空间缩小）；后处理阈值优化可消除25类设置下的差距；错误模式遵循CWE层次结构且与编码器选择高度相关（Pearson r > 0.92）；层次宽松评估将macro-F1从~81%提升至~90%；CySecBERT在多标签设置中表现最优。

Conclusion: CWE分类任务的错误结构主要由分类法设计驱动而非编码器选择；严格指标可能低估分支级分类器质量；多标签设置下领域专用编码器（CySecBERT）具有显著优势。

Abstract: Assigning Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) categories to Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) records remains an important but largely manual step in vulnerability analysis. We study this task as a text classification problem and compare two modelling choices: a \emph{multi-class} formulation that predicts a single CWE per CVE and a \emph{multi-label} formulation that allows multiple assignments. Three transformer encoders (BERT Base, SecureBERT, and CySecBERT) are evaluated on three nested label spaces (83, 47, and 25 classes). Multi-class training achieves higher macro-F1 across all settings, although the gap to multi-label narrows from 21 to 2 percentage points as the label space shrinks. Post-hoc threshold optimisation on the multi-label side closes this gap on the 25-class setting. Confusion analysis shows that the dominant misclassification patterns follow the CWE hierarchy and are shared across all three encoders (Pearson $r > 0.92$), which suggests that the error structure is driven more by taxonomy design than by encoder choice. A hierarchy-relaxed evaluation that forgives within-family confusions raises macro-F1 from ${\sim}$81\% to ${\sim}$90\%, indicating that strict metrics understate branch-level classifier quality. CySecBERT achieves the strongest results overall, with statistically significant gains concentrated in the multi-label setting.

</details>


### [209] [Asymmetric Focal Loss Improves Graph Neural Network Prediction of Drug-Drug Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07611)
*Faranak Hatami,Mousa Moradi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 ClinicalFocal loss 损失函数，替代标准二元交叉熵，用于图神经网络预测多关系药物-药物相互作用（DDI）。在 TWOSIDES 数据集上，使用关系感知图卷积网络，在相同条件下对比，ClinicalFocal loss 将准确率从 0.699 提升至 0.892（+19.3%），F1 从 0.700 提升至 0.894（+19.4%），AUROC 从 0.766 提升至 0.914，AUCPR 从 0.714 提升至 0.860，假阴性率从 29.8% 降至 9.1%，分类误差降低 64.1%。结论：不对称焦点优化在不改变架构的情况下显著改善了基于图的 DDI 预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准二元交叉熵训练将同等容量分配给易分类和难分类样本，可能遗漏临床显著相互作用。因此，评估不对称焦点目标（ClinicalFocal loss）能否通过强调难分类正样本来改进多关系药物-药物相互作用（DDI）预测。

Method: 将 ClinicalFocal loss 集成到关系感知图卷积网络中，使用分子指纹、理化描述符和学习嵌入。在 TWOSIDES 数据集上采用五折交叉验证，保持架构、特征、数据分区、超参数和随机种子完全一致，与二元交叉熵基线比较。

Result: ClinicalFocal loss 相比二元交叉熵：准确率从 0.699 升至 0.892（+19.3%），F1 从 0.700 升至 0.894（+19.4%），AUROC 从 0.766 升至 0.914，AUCPR 从 0.714 升至 0.860；假阴性率从 29.8% 降至 9.1%，特异性从 69.6% 升至 87.5%，总体分类误差从 30.1% 降至 10.8%（相对降低 64.1%），且五折结果一致。

Conclusion: 不对称焦点优化（ClinicalFocal loss）在不修改底层架构的情况下，显著改善了分类和排序性能，对观察到的相互作用三元组实现了 90.9% 的召回率。损失函数设计是改进基于图的 DDI 预测的一个直接、可调的控制手段。

Abstract: Background: Graph neural networks improve computational prediction of polypharmacy side effects, but standard binary cross-entropy training allocates equal capacity to well-classified and difficult examples, potentially missing clinically significant interactions. We evaluated whether an asymmetric focal objective could improve multi-relational drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction by emphasizing difficult positive interactions. Methods: ClinicalFocal loss was integrated into a relation-aware graph convolutional network using molecular fingerprints, physicochemical descriptors, and learned embeddings. The model was evaluated on TWOSIDES using five-fold cross-validation with identical experimental conditions (architecture, features, data partitions, hyperparameters, and random seeds) for ClinicalFocal loss and binary cross-entropy baseline. Results: ClinicalFocal loss increased accuracy from 0.699 to 0.892 (+19.3 percentage points) and F1 score from 0.700 to 0.894 (+19.4 percentage points). AUROC increased from 0.766 to 0.914, and AUCPR increased from 0.714 to 0.860. The false-negative rate decreased from 29.8% to 9.1%, while specificity increased from 69.6% to 87.5%. Overall classification error decreased from 30.1% to 10.8%, corresponding to a 64.1% relative reduction. Improvements were consistent across all five folds. Conclusions: Asymmetric focal optimization improved classification and ranking performance while achieving 90.9% recall for observed interaction triples, without modifying the underlying architecture. Loss-function design is a direct, tunable lever for improving graph-based DDI prediction.

</details>


### [210] [Higher-Order Geometric Updates for Levenberg-Marquardt Method via Riemann Normal Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07623)
*Jianing Liu,Dong H. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出黎曼正态坐标Levenberg-Marquardt方法（RNC-LM），通过引入任意阶测地线修正来消除参数效应曲率，使有限步优化具有更高的重参数化一致性。在经典非线性最小二乘基准、反应扩散PINN失效模式及大规模机器学习势能面拟合任务中，RNC-LM在收敛性、鲁棒性和计算效率上均显著优于标准LM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Levenberg-Marquardt方法的切空间步长在参数坐标中作为直线更新应用，这暴露了其局限性——参数效应曲率成为非线性的主要来源。测地线加速度虽能提供二阶修正，但仅在无穷小步长下精确消除参数效应曲率。对于有限步优化，需要更好的重参数化一致性。

Method: 通过重新表述测地线方程，将测地线加速度推广到任意阶修正，构建具有渐进高阶重参数化一致性的有限步更新。沿所得到的RNC曲线进行线搜索控制步长，同时在移动切框架中逐阶消除残差加速度的切向分量。

Result: 在经典非线性最小二乘基准上改善了弯曲谷和秩亏问题的收敛性与鲁棒性；在反应扩散PINN失效模式基准上，将相对L2误差降低至10^{-3}量级并恢复物理有意义的解；在大规模机器学习势能面拟合任务中，相比标准LM实现了34倍加速。

Conclusion: RNC-LM通过消除参数效应曲率的影响，使实际目标函数下降与LM的线性模型预测更加一致，显著提升了LM方法在处理强非线性问题时的性能。

Abstract: Nonlinear least-squares optimization is central to regression, physics-informed neural networks, and other machine-learning tasks. Such problems have a natural geometric interpretation, model predictions form a manifold in data space, while the chosen parameterization can introduce parameter-effects curvature that becomes a dominant source of nonlinearity. This exposes a limitation of the Levenberg-Marquardt (LM) method, its tangent-space step is applied as a straight update in parameter coordinates. Geodesic acceleration gives a second-order correction, but its removal of parameter-effect curvature is exact only in the infinitesimal-step limit. We propose a Riemann-normal-coordinate Levenberg-Marquardt method (RNC-LM) to improve this consistency for finite optimization steps. By reformulating the geodesic equation, RNC-LM extends geodesic acceleration to arbitrary-order corrections and constructs finite-step updates with progressively higher reparameterization consistency. A line search along the resulting RNC curve controls the traveled distance while keeping the cost close to standard LM. The method eliminates the tangential component of residual acceleration order by order in a moving tangent frame, making the actual objective reduction more consistent with the linear model prediction of LM. On classical nonlinear least-squares benchmarks, RNC-LM improves convergence and robustness in curved valleys and rank-deficient problems. On a reaction-diffusion PINN failure-mode benchmark, it reduces the relative L2 error to the order of 1e-3 and recovers a physically meaningful solution. On a large-scale machine-learning potential-energy-surface fitting task, it achieves a 34-fold speedup over standard LM.

</details>


### [211] [An optimal control approach for neural network architecture adaptation with a posteriori error estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07637)
*C G Krishnanunni,Thomas Scott,Tan Bui-Thanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于后验误差估计的神经网络深度自适应方法，将网络训练建模为连续时间最优控制问题，通过导出可计算的误差上界实现层结构的精细化调整，在科学数据集上取得了优于现有方法的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络架构自适应方法缺乏对近似误差在各层之间分布的定量分析，无法精确指导网络深度调整。作者希望建立严格的误差估计框架，使网络能够根据误差分布有针对性地添加层，从而高效捕捉底层问题的复杂非线性变化。

Method: 将神经网络训练重新表述为连续时间最优控制问题；利用有限元分析中的对偶加权残量方法导出可计算的泛函误差上界；将总近似误差分解为区间（层）贡献，识别误差最大的位置；在误差最大处插入新层；权值和偏置被建模为跨层分段线性函数，误差估计器用于量化离散表示与真连续最优控制解之间的差异。

Result: 在科学数据集（包括Navier-Stokes方程的可观测量到参数映射学习任务）上，所提出的方法在泛化性能上持续优于现有的架构自适应方法。

Conclusion: 基于后验误差估计的深度自适应策略是有效的，能为神经网络架构设计提供严格的理论基础，尤其在科学计算等对精度要求高的任务中具有显著优势。

Abstract: This work presents a novel approach for adapting neural network architecture along the depth based on a posteriori error estimation. By formulating neural network training as a continuous-time optimal control problem, we derive rigorous error estimates that quantify how approximation error distributes across network layers. This error decomposition enables a principled depth adaptation strategy: new layers are inserted at locations of maximum estimated error, allowing the network to efficiently capture complex, nonlinear variations in the underlying problem. Our framework introduces a novel network architecture that treats weights and biases as piecewise linear functions varying across layers, with the error estimator bounding the discrepancy between this discrete representation and the true continuous optimal control solution. The approach leverages dual weighted residual methodology from finite element analysis to derive computable upper bounds on the functional error. A key theoretical contribution is the derivation of explicit error bounds that decompose the total approximation error into interval-wise contributions, providing a rigorous basis for targeted architecture refinement. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on scientific datasets, including learning the observable-to-parameter map for the Navier-Stokes equation. Numerical results reveal that our approach consistently outperforms existing architecture adaptation methods in terms of generalization performance.

</details>


### [212] [ALER-TI: Aligned Latent Embedding Retrieval for Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07640)
*Xuan-Thong Truong,Trung-Kien Le,Tung Kieu,Thi-Thu Nguyen,Nhat-Hai Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ALER-TI是一个检索增强的时间序列插补框架，通过历史模式补充局部上下文以重建缺失值，并提出潜在嵌入对齐（LEA）缓解查询与候选之间的表示不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列插补方法主要依赖损坏输入序列中的局部时间上下文，这在现实场景中面临非平稳动态、弱时间相关性和低频模式等限制，难以仅从邻近观测中可靠重构。

Method: 提出ALER-TI框架，核心是潜在嵌入对齐（LEA），通过在后验空间中应用遮蔽操作，将候选嵌入与查询的缺失模式对齐，并允许历史嵌入预计算缓存以实现高效检索；该框架模型无关，可通过轻量适配模块集成多种插补骨干。

Result: 在六个真实世界数据集和不同缺失率下的实验表明，ALER-TI持续改进强基线模型，并增强了跨不同插补设置的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ALER-TI通过显式利用历史模式补充退化局部上下文，有效提升了时间序列插补性能，且具有良好的通用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deep learning has significantly advanced time series imputation, yet most existing architectures primarily rely on localized temporal context within the corrupted input sequence. This reliance can be limiting in real-world scenarios, where time series often exhibit non-stationary dynamics, weak temporal correlations, and infrequent patterns that are difficult to reconstruct from nearby observations alone. In this paper, we propose ALER-TI, Aligned Latent Embedding Retrieval for Time Series Imputation, a retrieval-augmented framework that explicitly leverages historical patterns to supplement degraded local context for more reliable missing-value reconstruction. The core of ALER-TI is Latent Embedding Alignment (LEA), which mitigates the representation mismatch between corrupted queries and complete historical candidates. By applying post-hoc masking in the latent space, LEA aligns candidates with the query's missingness pattern while allowing historical embeddings to be pre-computed and cached for efficient retrieval. ALER-TI is model-agnostic and can be integrated with various imputation backbones through a lightweight adaptation module. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets under different missing rates demonstrate that ALER-TI consistently improves strong baseline models and enhances robustness across diverse imputation settings.

</details>


### [213] [Guidance Breaks the Fitted Operator: A Terminal-Fitted Repair for Classifier-Free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07665)
*Shiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了无分类器引导（CFG）在扩散模型中的过饱和和不稳定问题，发现CFG会使判别子空间重新刚化，导致DDIM步长不再是拟合算子，且引导残差随sigma_min减小而发散。作者提出了一种零额外NFE的修复方法：将CFG的w(r-1)替换为r^(1+w)-r，该修正消除了CFG的发散性，在低分辨率和高分辨率模型上均能稳定高引导强度，且不增加计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFG在引导强度过大时会出现过饱和和不稳定，通常需要增加步数或使用限制区间调度来抑制。作者希望通过数值分析视角理解CFG的渐近保持性质，并找到一种无需额外计算成本就能稳定高引导强度的方法。

Method: 作者从数值分析角度分析CFG，基于DDIM步骤是未引导终端层的唯一拟合算子这一结果，证明引导重新刚化判别子空间至异常指数1+w。通过推导引导钟形障碍（具有三个有序步长阈值），将单步过饱和解读为解算器伪影而非连续引导规律。提出的修复方法是将CFG的引导方向从w(r-1)改为r^(1+w)-r，消除了sigma_min趋近于零时的发散，并在校准模型的判别交叉上达到一阶精度。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和Stable Diffusion 1.5 DDIM上，该方法作为高引导稳定器，减少了残差放大和饱和，在测试网格上9/9点FID优于CFG，在硬单元块中保持了分类器代理目标精度。但并非通用的图像质量改进，也不是比密集的vanilla-CFG参考更好的统一积分器。

Conclusion: 提出的修复方法有效稳定了高引导强度，但有其局限性：不是通用的图像质量提升，也不是在所有情况下都比CFG更好的积分器。未来可能需要进一步研究更通用的方法。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is the standard way to strengthen class-conditioning in diffusion and flow-matching samplers, yet at large guidance it oversaturates and destabilizes, symptoms practitioners suppress with more steps or limited-interval schedules. We analyze CFG through an asymptotic-preserving, numerical-analysis lens. Building on a recent result that the deterministic DDIM step is the unique fitted operator for the unguided terminal layer, exact on the final small-sigma stretch of sampling, we show that guidance re-stiffens exactly the discriminative subspace to an anomalous exponent 1+w. DDIM is therefore no longer fitted there, and on coarse meshes its guided residual diverges as sigma_min goes to zero. We prove a guided clock barrier with three ordered step-size thresholds, and read one-step oversaturation as its endpoint: a solver artifact on the calibration model rather than the continuous guided law. The same analysis yields a one-coefficient, zero-extra-NFE repair: replace CFG's w(r-1) by r^(1+w)-r on the guidance direction. On the calibration model's discriminative crossover, this removes CFG's sigma_min-divergent blow-up and is first-order accurate against the exact guided flow as sigma_min goes to zero. On learned CIFAR-10 checkpoints, and as a cross-domain smoke test on Stable Diffusion 1.5 DDIM, it acts as a high-guidance stabilizer at no extra cost rather than a universal quality knob: it cuts residual amplification and saturation, gives 9/9 point-FID wins over CFG on the tested grid, and preserves classifier-proxy target accuracy in the hard-cell blocks. We report the limits alongside: it is not a universal image-quality win, and against a dense vanilla-CFG reference it is not a uniformly better integrator of that field.

</details>


### [214] [PeTeR: Post-Training Robustification of Probabilistic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07671)
*Adrian Ciotinga,Yeming Dai,YooJung Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PeTeR，一种无需数据的后训练框架，通过分布鲁棒优化增强预训练概率电路对分布偏移的鲁棒性，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准似然学习的概率电路在面对数据噪声、小样本或分布偏移时容易过拟合和泛化脆弱，现有分布鲁棒优化方法需要从头训练，成本高。

Method: 提出PeTeR（Post-Training Robustification），一种无需数据的后训练框架，在Wasserstein球内考虑最坏情况分布来鲁棒化预训练的概率电路。

Result: 在多个密度估计基准上，PeTeR有效提升了基线模型对随机和对抗扰动的鲁棒性，性能达到或超过需要数据的鲁棒学习方法。

Conclusion: PeTeR是一种高效、无需数据的后训练鲁棒化方法，能够在不重新训练的前提下增强预训练PC的分布鲁棒性。

Abstract: Probabilistic circuits (PCs) can model complex joint distributions while supporting exact and efficient computation of many inference queries. However, standard likelihood-based PC learning is vulnerable to overfitting and fragile generalization when confronted with data noise, small sample sizes, or distribution shifts. This can be mitigated using distributionally-robust optimization which consider worst-case distributions within a Wasserstein ball of the empirical distribution, but current methods are limited to training a model from scratch in this framework. Instead, we propose PeTeR: a novel, data-free post-training framework designed to robustify pre-trained PCs against distribution shifts without retraining from scratch.
